## Review note — 2026-10-26

This current-runner attempt is **FAIL / not a completed H04 qualification**. After the panel pause, the instrument returned `VOLT? = 1` for CH1, not the expected clamped `2.00 V`; the runner stopped before checking CH2/CH3 and subsequent H04 cases. The SYST:ERR? response was `0, "No error"`, and instrument readbacks showed all outputs OFF. The operator clarified that Max Volt Set had not been changed and remained `4 V` on all channels; the earlier panel token was premature and is not evidence of clamp. Final OFF state was independently confirmed by the operator. H04 stays NOT_RUN pending corrected replay.
